# How this repository was made (Norway)

This file is written by the build on 2026-11-02, the day the source data was retrieved.

## Sources

| Source | Address | Retrieved | Licence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Norsk Lovtidend avd. I (Lovdata public data) | <https://api.lovdata.no/publicData> | 2026-10-29 | NLOD 2.0 |
| Gjeldende lover (Lovdata public data) | <https://api.lovdata.no/v1/publicData/get/gjeldende-lover.tar.bz2> | 2026-10-29 | NLOD 2.0 |
| Gjeldende sentrale forskrifter (Lovdata public data) | <https://api.lovdata.no/v1/publicData/get/gjeldende-sentrale-forskrifter.tar.bz2> | 2026-11-02 | NLOD 2.0 |
| Lovdata API description and data set list | <https://api.lovdata.no/swagger/api.json> | 2026-10-29 | NLOD 2.0 |

Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (Europe/Oslo) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Norway has no single default commencement rule in this record: each in-force day is the one Lovdata records for the official Norsk Lovtidend document ("I kraft fra"; for a decision amending the Constitution, the day of Stortinget's decision), and where a law leaves commencement to the King ("Kongen bestemmer"), the day Lovdata's amendment note gives for that provision ("ikr. ... iflg. res. ..."). https://api.lovdata.no/xmldocs

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `fe52f9323bc286b72b872dadd86b7dbc7e5e00c238f7b3f128d0c9c9b5ba70d8` (84204064 bytes).
- Builder blob hashes (`git hash-object`): `build.py` `900da0d33bfd1ef455267fb4dcef7961fd5acc9c`, `check.py` `5d719354ea4c62d240c93d2cae446307d81d9e81`, `on.py` `aff48cf1b68d50c7ba8f399ca32261871c301824`.
- Python 3.14.4; git version 2.53.0.

## Decisions made by hand

- Text comes only from Norsk Lovtidend avd. I, the official announcement of Norwegian law; Lovdata's consolidated text (unofficial) is used only to check the replay and for in-force days of provisions whose law left commencement to the King.
- Grunnloven and Grunnlova are two acts: since 6 May 2014 the Constitution has official bokmål and nynorsk texts of equal standing, and every amendment is announced in both.
- Grunnloven starts on 2014-05-06 with the full text of the language revision announced by res. 9 May 2014 no. 613, as corrected by Lovdata on 2014-05-30 (bokmål §§ 2, 6, 53, 63, 92, 112). Earlier wording is in the old language and the full text before 2014 is not in the open archive, so this is the oldest version available (first_law null).
- The decision of 13 May 2014 (res. 14 May 2014 no. 628) moved provisions to a new chapter F "with a new paragraph number" it did not give. The 13 May version therefore uses the numbering and text of chapters E and F in the edited version Stortinget adopted on 27 May 2014 (res. 20 June 2014 no. 778), and the 27 May commit changes no words.
- Amendment instructions are applied by code: ledd and punktum being replaced or repealed count the text as it read before; "nytt" ledd and punktum name the new position; list letters are addressed by letter. An instruction the code cannot read stops the build.
- Grunnloven: amendments of 2014-2024 are dated by Stortinget's decision, the "I kraft fra" day Lovdata gives each announcement.
- Repealed paragraphs are removed files; the editorial "(Opphevet ved ...)" lines Lovdata prints are not law text and are left out.
- Chapter headings are units of their own (kapittel-*.md).
- List items are written "a) text", in one form for both sources (Lovtidend prints "a)", the consolidated text "a.").
- Number ranges are written with a hyphen (§§ 77-79): Lovdata prints an en dash in some texts and a hyphen in others.
- personopplysningsloven § 34 (Endringer i andre lover) keeps only its opening sentence, as in Lovdata's consolidated text: the amendments it made live on in the acts they amended.
- personopplysningsloven: the annexed Norwegian text of the GDPR (EEA version), which § 1 makes law, is not included; only the act's own chapters 1-9 are.
- personopplysningsloven: both amending laws left commencement to the King; each change is dated by the in-force day in Lovdata's note for the provision (2018-12-20 for § 12 a, 2022-01-01 for § 3), which matches the commencement resolutions FOR-2018-12-20-2093 and FOR-2021-06-18-1967.
- Ordinary acts: the amending laws are every law Lovtidend's metadata ("Endrer") lists as changing the act, replayed in promulgation order; no law Lovdata's notes name is missing from them.
- A change Lovdata's note gives no in-force day for took effect with the amending law itself: it is dated by the first day in that law's "I kraft fra" field, and never before the act itself took effect (arveloven: amendments of 18 December 2020 to the act that took effect on 1 January 2021 are same-day versions on that day).
- A change Lovdata marks "Endres ved lov ... (i kraft fra den tid Kongen bestemmer)" has no in-force day yet, so it is not in the history: arveloven § 103 and § 178 (lov 19. juni 2026 nr. 48).
- Word substitutions ("I følgende bestemmelser skal «fylkesmannen» endres til «statsforvalteren»") are applied to the paragraphs and ledd they name, in lower and capitalised form; inflected forms that contain the word (fylkesmannens) change with it.
- Lovdata's editorial mark ": – – –" after "gjøres følgende endringer i andre lover" is not compared.
- sha256 of a version is the sha256 of the single Lovtidend XML document (the tar member named after # in source_url) it was parsed from.
- andsverkloven: replayed from the act as promulgated (LOV-2018-06-15-40) through every amending law Norsk Lovtidend lists for it; the replay matches Lovdata's consolidated text (as of 2026-10-21) in every unit (140).
- andsverkloven: paragraf-119 (amendments to other acts): the consolidated text of Lovdata prints this paragraph as "– – –"; here, as for every such paragraph, only the opening words are kept.
- arbeidsmiljoloven: replayed from the act as promulgated (LOV-2005-06-17-62) through every amending law Norsk Lovtidend lists for it; the replay matches Lovdata's consolidated text (as of 2026-10-21) in every unit (213).
- arbeidsmiljoloven: Lov 19. juni 2026 nr. 48 is passed but not yet in force (Lovdata: "i kraft fra den tid Kongen bestemmer"); its changes are not in the history: paragraf-14-002.
- arbeidsmiljoloven: amending instruction not applied by the replay (LOV-2009-06-19-74, it stands in another act's list of amendments to other acts, which take effect with that act ("Med virkning fra den tid hele straffeloven 20. mai 2005 nr. 28 settes i kraft, gjøres følgende endringer i annen lovgivning"); Lovdata credits this law with no change to the act): § 19-2 første ledd annet punktum oppheves.
- arbeidsmiljoloven: amending instruction not applied by the replay (LOV-2009-06-19-74, it stands in another act's list of amendments to other acts, which take effect with that act ("Med virkning fra den tid hele straffeloven 20. mai 2005 nr. 28 settes i kraft, gjøres følgende endringer i annen lovgivning"); Lovdata credits this law with no change to the act): § 19-4 nytt annet punktum skal lyde:
- arbeidsmiljoloven: amending instruction not applied by the replay (LOV-2009-06-19-74, it stands in another act's list of amendments to other acts, which take effect with that act ("Med virkning fra den tid hele straffeloven 20. mai 2005 nr. 28 settes i kraft, gjøres følgende endringer i annen lovgivning"); Lovdata credits this law with no change to the act): § 19-5 skal lyde:
- arbeidsmiljoloven: amending instruction not applied by the replay (LOV-2009-06-19-74, it stands in another act's list of amendments to other acts, which take effect with that act ("Med virkning fra den tid hele straffeloven 20. mai 2005 nr. 28 settes i kraft, gjøres følgende endringer i annen lovgivning"); Lovdata credits this law with no change to the act): § 19-7 oppheves.

- arbeidsmiljoloven: § 10-11: the law moves ledd past one another (Nåværende § 10-11 tredje ledd blir femte ledd.; Nåværende § 10-11 femte til åttende ledd blir sjette til niende ledd.); the ledd are placed by their new numbers, and its later instructions on them use the new numbers (Lov 16. juni 2017 nr. 42).

- finansforetaksloven: Lov 22. desember 2021 nr. 163 added the heading of kapittel-16-avsnitt-02; no in-force provision names the heading, so it is dated with the law's changes under it, 2022-01-01.
- finansforetaksloven: Lov 19. juni 2026 nr. 40 puts its change of § 2-1 in force only for the third ledd on 2026-07-01 (the commencement resolution); the rest of its change of § 2-1 waits for a day the King sets and is not in the history.
- finansforetaksloven: Lov 19. juni 2026 nr. 40 puts its change of § 8-5 in force only for the fourth ledd on 2026-07-01 (the commencement resolution); the rest of its change of § 8-5 waits for a day the King sets and is not in the history.
- finansforetaksloven: Lov 6. februar 2026 nr. 2 ("§ 2-1 tredje ledd bokstav f og ny bokstav g skal lyde") was written for a text in which § 2-1 third ledd had no bokstav g. It took effect on 2026-08-01, when the text in force, through a law promulgated later but in force earlier, had one; its words are applied literally to the text then in force, each letter it names set as it says, so bokstav g is replaced rather than added, as in the consolidated text of Lovdata. The record follows the laws as written and does not correct them. Lovdata notes: "Inkurie: Tidligere bokstav f ble ved en inkurie overskrevet av endring ved lov 6. februar 2026 nr. 2 fra 1. august 2026, som ikke tok høyde for omnummerering av punktene gjennomført ved lov 19. juni 2026 nr. 40 fra 1. juli 2026. Bokstav f hadde følgende ordlyd: «virksomhet som finansagent eller finansrådgiver»."
- finansforetaksloven: some amending laws took effect before laws promulgated earlier (§ 2-1 by Lov 6. februar 2026 nr. 2); the amendments are applied in the order they took effect, each to the text in force on its day.

- plan-og-bygningsloven: paragraf-35-001 (amendments to other acts): the consolidated text of Lovdata prints this paragraph as "– – –" with fragments of the amendments; here only its title is kept, as it has no words of its own.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: § 35-1 (amendments to other acts) has no words of its own, only numbered amendments of other acts and their new texts; as for every such paragraph, those are not kept here (they live on in the acts they amended), so it holds only its title.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 8. mai 2009 nr. 27 took effect on § 23-7 on 2010-07-01 without its first ledd second sentence, which was postponed with no day set and is not in the history.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 8. mai 2009 nr. 27 took effect on § 24-1 piece by piece: first ledd letter a on 2013-01-01; its other words on 2010-07-01; until each piece's day the earlier wording of that piece stands (a new provision: before its day the piece is not there).
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 8. mai 2009 nr. 27 left its commencement to the King; its changes are dated by the commencement resolutions FOR-2009-06-12-638, FOR-2009-06-26-859, FOR-2010-06-18-896, FOR-2011-04-29-479, FOR-2012-06-22-574: the changes in chapters 1 and 19 and in § 34-2 fifth ledd from 1 July 2009, § 24-2 and § 24-1 first ledd letter a (postponed twice) from 1 January 2013, § 23-7 first ledd second sentence postponed with no day set, everything else from 1 July 2010.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lovdata lists Lov 20. juni 2014 nr. 52 on § 23-7; its new wording is the wording already in force, without the first ledd second sentence of Lov 8. mai 2009 nr. 27 that never took effect, so it changes nothing here.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 20. juni 2014 nr. 52 took effect on § 32-8 piece by piece: letter a on 2014-06-20; letter b on 2014-06-20; the rest of the first ledd on 2015-11-13; first ledd letter e on 2016-01-01; until each piece's day the earlier wording of that piece stands.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 20. juni 2014 nr. 52 changed the heading of the fourth part; no in-force provision names the heading, so it is dated with the law's changes under it (in force on 2014-06-20, 2015-07-01, 2015-11-13, 2016-01-01; the heading on the last day), 2016-01-01.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 20. juni 2014 nr. 52 changed the heading of chapter 22; no in-force provision names the heading, so it is dated with the law's changes under it, 2016-01-01.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 21. juni 2017 nr. 97 inserts a new seventh ledd in § 21-7 and renumbers the ledd after it, numbered by the text before an amendment made by another law that took effect on the same day. Read literally, that numbering would duplicate an existing ledd: it makes the present seventh ledd the eighth, but the text in force already has an eighth ledd, which the law does not renumber. Lovdata's note resolves it: the new ledd is now the eighth ledd, and is inserted there.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 26. mai 2020 nr. 50 is a temporary law (in force 2020-05-26 to 2021-01-01); § 20-9 it added is in force for that time and then removed, both credited to it.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 18. desember 2020 nr. 151 is a temporary law (in force 2021-01-01 to 2021-06-01); § 20-9 it added is in force for that time and then removed, both credited to it.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 28. mai 2021 nr. 48 is a temporary law (in force 2021-06-01 to 2021-12-01); § 20-9 it added is in force for that time and then removed, both credited to it.
- plan-og-bygningsloven: Lov 10. juni 2022 nr. 35 says "Del III til X a i loven oppheves 1. juli 2024." (as Lov 2. juni 2023 nr. 17 worded it). Lovdata also lists that law as repealing § 20-9 on 2024-07-01. The repeal of an amending law's own parts after they took effect does not undo the amendments they made, which are spent: § 20-9 stays.

- tvisteloven: amending instruction not applied by the replay (LOV-2023-12-20-110, not read): instruction not read: amends the change an earlier amending law made to § 4-4, before that change took effect: I del II i endringen av lov 17. juni 2005 nr. 90 om mekling og rettergang i sivile tvister § 4-4 oppheves
- tvisteloven: kapittel-09 is a chapter heading the official Lovtidend text prints without a full stop after the chapter number, where the consolidated text of Lovdata has one; the official wording is kept.
- tvisteloven: paragraf-37-003 (amendments to other acts): the consolidated text of Lovdata prints this paragraph as "– – –" with fragments of the amendments; here only its title is kept, as it has no words of its own.
- tvisteloven: § 37-3 (amendments to other acts) has no words of its own, only numbered amendments of other acts and their new texts; as for every such paragraph, those are not kept here (they live on in the acts they amended), so it holds only its title.
- tvisteloven: Lov 17. juni 2016 nr. 55 took effect on § 4-4 only for its fifth and sixth ledd (Lovdata: "gjelder ... ledd"); its change of the fourth ledd never took effect and is left out.
- tvisteloven: § 13-7: Norsk Lovtidend prints the title of the new text as a first line of text after a heading that holds only the number; it is read as the title (Lov 22. juni 2018 nr. 79).
- tvisteloven: § 10-2: the instruction "§ 10-2 nytt tredje og fjerde ledd skal lyde:" gives 2 new ledd, the last a revision of the ledd now at that place; that ledd is replaced by it, as in the consolidated text of Lovdata (Lov 11. mai 2023 nr. 13).
- tvisteloven: Lov 5. april 2013 nr. 12 replaced the list of amendments to other acts in Lov 26. mars 2010 nr. 9 before that law took effect; the amendments of this act are applied as restated, with that law, on 2013-07-01, credited to both laws (§ 2-2, § 2-3, § 2-4).
- tvisteloven: Lov 18. desember 2020 nr. 149 replaced the list of amendments to other acts in Lov 14. juni 2019 nr. 21 before that law took effect; the amendments of this act are applied as restated, with that law, on 2021-01-01, credited to both laws (§ 6-2).
- tvisteloven: Lov 21. juni 2024 nr. 46 replaced the list of amendments to other acts in Lov 12. mai 2022 nr. 28 before that law took effect; the amendments of this act are applied as restated, with that law, on 2025-01-01, credited to both laws (§ 3-3, § 6-7, § 22-5).

- Current-text acts (6 in this part): each is one version, Lovdata's consolidated text (unofficial) as of 2026-11-01, credited to Lovdata as the oldest version available and dated by the day Lovdata gives for the last change in force (or its own in-force day). Each act's units hold the consolidated document's text word for word (Lovdata's editorial notes, footnotes, the title line and repealed "(Opphevet)" articles apart; images are not included): paragraphs, articles ("Art.") and headings as the document numbers them, or one unit holding the whole text of a document with no numbered articles (a single-file act).

- Current-text regulations (73 in this part): each is one version, Lovdata's consolidated text (unofficial) as retrieved on 2026-11-02, credited to Lovdata as the oldest version available and dated by the day Lovdata gives for the last change in force (or its own in-force day). Each act's units hold the consolidated document's text word for word (Lovdata's editorial notes, footnotes, the title line and repealed "(Opphevet)" articles apart; images are not included): paragraphs, articles ("Art.") and headings as the document numbers them, or one unit holding the whole text of a document with no numbered articles (a single-file act).

- Current-text regulations (2 in this part): each is one version, Lovdata's consolidated text (unofficial) as retrieved on 2026-11-03, credited to Lovdata as the oldest version available and dated by the day Lovdata gives for the last change in force (or its own in-force day). Each act's units hold the consolidated document's text word for word (Lovdata's editorial notes, footnotes, the title line and repealed "(Opphevet)" articles apart; images are not included): paragraphs, articles ("Art.") and headings as the document numbers them, or one unit holding the whole text of a document with no numbered articles (a single-file act).

## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- Claude Code (Claude Opus 5.5, claude-opus-5-5) wrote Lexifina's code and read sample documents to design it. No model wrote, edited or summarised legal text, and no model runs at data-build or git-build time.
